# Forward audit — M6.P2 T40, the patches and kernel configurations no plan applies (#687)

**The read:** the 778 rows at `8ebaf8daad` (unchanged at the dispatch tip `b8cde6f305`); T23's table from `feature/m6-packages-dead-ends-670` at `f1561559aa`, read with `git show`. **Depth:** Local, no reviewer seat; the counts are the check. **The rule (D-WORKFLOW-209):** no `.patch`, `.conf`, recipe or tool changes on this branch. The tables come from `tools/t40-tables.py`, which asserts 778, 317,908, 760 and 18.

**Can this be done on the VM? Yes -- on the host, with no network at all.** The inputs are the inventory, the patch headers, `config/options ""` and `get_pkg_directory` (run with a logging `curl` shim on `PATH`: 0 calls), `scripts/unpack` read, `git log` and `git show`. There is no guest, no build and no plan generation. Whether a renamed patch applies and compiles is a package build's to show, an open item for the next image (D-QA-051).

## Classes

The first matching rule decides each row's class. Two commands measure one quantity: the inventory's `reach: unreached` on all 778, and the lane's class script over `unpack`'s rules and T23's units. Every row is classed by the script; none is left unclassed.

| Class | Rule | Rows | Lines | Disposition |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| (a) | under `packages/linux/patches/`; `get_pkg_directory linux` resolves to `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/linux` on every device, and the generic directory is chosen only by another project's `LINUX=` | 277 | 92,389 | dead: a generic kernel patch the ROCKNIX override never applies |
| (b) | under `projects/<not ROCKNIX>/`; the Makefile sets `PROJECT=ROCKNIX` on all 27 build lines | 234 | 160,488 | dead: another project's, which no Makefile target builds |
| (c) | under `projects/ROCKNIX/.../patches/x86_64/`; `unpack:127-128` sets `PATCH_ARCH=x86` | 5 | 99 | never selected: a `patches/x86_64` directory under `PATCH_ARCH=x86` (the bug) |
| (d) | the nearest recipe directory is one of T23's 731 units | 234 | 63,795 | inside a T23 dead unit (its T23 disposition carried); 6 of them already deleted at `upstream/next` |
| (e1) | a generic recipe a live override sources | 6 | 153 | 4 reached after all (gcc 3 and cmake 1, through the symlinked `patches`); 2 dead (fluidsynth, readline) |
| (e2) | an unbuilt recipe's (the 16 LibreELEC `libretro-*`, 2 ROCKNIX-own, 4 dvb and firmware) | 22 | 984 | dead: an unbuilt recipe's |
| **Total** | | **778** | **317,908** | |

**By disposition** (rows): 277 kernel; 234 other projects; 5 never selected; 152 inside a T23 *dead, upstream-carried* unit; 67 inside a *shadowed copy, override live* unit; 9 inside a *shadowed copy, override dead too* unit; **6 already deleted at `upstream/next`**; 2 sourced generic recipes' patches; 22 unbuilt recipes'; **4 reached after all**. The sum is 778.

## The x86_64 five

| File | Lines | Its recipe | Header |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/emulators/libretro/vitaquake2-lr/patches/x86_64/001-set-platform.patch` | 18 | live (reachable by H700 and SM8550; buildable by AMD64) | hand-made |
| `.../vitaquake2-lr/vitaquake2-rogue-lr/patches/x86_64/001-set-platform.patch` | 18 | the same | hand-made |
| `.../vitaquake2-lr/vitaquake2-xatrix-lr/patches/x86_64/001-set-platform.patch` | 18 | the same | hand-made |
| `.../vitaquake2-lr/vitaquake2-zaero-lr/patches/x86_64/001-set-platform.patch` | 18 | the same | hand-made |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/ui/emulationstation/patches/x86_64/cpugov.patch` | 27 | live (reached by GENERIC_X64) | hand-made; added by `40fce2a089` (2026-08-20, *amd64: wip am64 changes*) |

**The trace:**
- `scripts/unpack:127-128` (at `8ebaf8daad`):

  ```text
      if [ "${TARGET_ARCH}" = "x86_64" ]; then
        PATCH_ARCH="x86"
  ```

  The patch loop then globs `${PKG_DIR}/patches/${PATCH_ARCH}/*.patch`, so a directory named `x86_64` is never read. The convention is deliberate (`711b5fc4c3`, 2014-05-24, *scripts/unpack: add support for arch specific patches*). **No recipe in the tree has a `patches/x86/` directory, and these five are the only `patches/x86_64/` files.** The arch-named directories are 11 `aarch64`, 2 `arm` and 5 `x86_64`.
- `scripts/unpack:196` substitutes before applying:

  ```text
            patch -d "${PKG_BUILD}" -p1 < <(sed -e "s#@TARGET_CPU@#${TARGET_CPU}#g" -e "s#@DEVICE@#${DEVICE}#g" "${i}") >&"${VERBOSE_OUT}"
  ```

- **vitaquake2-lr's `x86_64` patch against its live `aarch64` one:** one hunk, differing in four lines.

  ```text
  < @@ -76,6 +76,15 @@         > @@ -76,6 +76,14 @@
  < +else ifeq ($(platform), @DEVICE@_rocknix)    > +else ifeq ($(platform), @DEVICE@)
  < +    fpic := -fPIC          > +        fpic := -fPIC
  < +    CFLAGS += -ffast-math -DARM
  < +    CFLAGS += -D_POSIX_C_SOURCE=200809L -D_DEFAULT_SOURCE    > +    CFLAGS += -D_POSIX_C_SOURCE=199309L
  ```

  **A rename alone would not make it take effect.** `pre_make_target` (`vitaquake2-lr/package.mk:19-25`) passes `platform=` only on aarch64:

  ```text
    case ${TARGET_ARCH} in
      aarch64) PKG_MAKE_OPTS_TARGET+=" platform=${DEVICE}_rocknix" ;;
    esac
  ```

  On x86_64 no `platform=` is passed, so the patched `@DEVICE@` branch would never be chosen. It has been unapplied since `9f1fab30f6` (2025-07-13). No image the fork tests builds vitaquake2-lr on x86_64 (GENERIC_X64 does not plan it).
- **The `cpugov.patch` slip:** the patch targets `es-app/src/ApiSystem.cpp`, sourcing `/etc/profile.d/100-amd64-freqfunctions`, which the ROCKNIX `rocknix` package installs (`projects/ROCKNIX/packages/rocknix/profile.d/100-amd64-freqfunctions`). Its `getAvailableGpuGovernors()` calls `get_available_cpu_governors` in **both** arms (patch lines 21 and 23); upstream ES called `get_available_gpu_governors` (line 19, removed). Applied, the GPU governor list would read the CPU's. It never has been.
- **The fix is not made here:** a rename to `patches/x86`, the slip corrected, and vitaquake2-lr's `pre_make_target` given an x86_64 branch. It is a packet after the maintainer's word. Whether the renamed EmulationStation patch applies and runs is the next image's open item; vitaquake2-lr's needs an AMD64 build nobody makes.

## The symlinked four

| Row | The override's `patches` | `ls-tree` mode | Applied by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `packages/lang/gcc/patches/0001-4.8.2-disable-multilib-i386-linux64.patch` | `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/lang/gcc/patches -> ../../../../../packages/lang/gcc/patches/` | `120000` | `unpack`'s `${PKG_DIR}/patches/*.patch`, with `PKG_DIR` the override |
| `packages/lang/gcc/patches/0002-allow-newer-autoconf.patch` | the same | `120000` | the same |
| `packages/lang/gcc/patches/0003-crosscompile-badness.patch` | the same | `120000` | the same |
| `packages/devel/cmake/patches/0001-disable-free-comp-methods.patch` | `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/devel/cmake/patches -> ../../../../../packages/devel/cmake/patches/` | `120000` | the same |

`gcc` and `cmake` are in every image's plan, so these four are applied on every build while the inventory reads them `unreached`. `tools/renovation-inventory`'s `selected_patch_dirs` (`:382-401`) records the override's `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/lang/gcc/patches`, normalized with `os.path.normpath` (`:397`), which does not follow a symlink. Git tracks the files only at `packages/lang/gcc/patches/` (the symlink is a blob), and a patch row's reach is `reached_pdir.get(<its directory>)` (`:461-462`), so the generic directory is never in the set and the four read `unreached`. The fix resolves the link (`os.path.realpath` against the checkout, or the blob's target). That is a second gap beside #1049, filed as a `bug` at stage D with a constructed case.

## Provenance

One row per path (778). The columns:
- path, lines, kind, class;
- the unit (the nearest recipe directory), with its T23 disposition for class (d);
- `shadowed_by`;
- the header's origin (`From:`) and subject, or `hand-made`;
- the subject's tag, the series position, and the first target (`diff --git a/`);
- the upstream status: carried; deleted at `upstream/next` since the base; the override carries the same change; or a LibreELEC change the override builds without;
- the disposition.

## The runs

| Run | Result | A FAIL would mean |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `get_pkg_directory linux` under each device's `config/options ""`: GENERIC_X64/x86_64, H700/aarch64, SM8550/aarch64, H700/arm, SM8550/arm | rc 0, 1 s; all five `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/linux`; 0 `curl` calls; the worktree's ignored set unchanged | a device builds the generic kernel, so class (a) would be live there |
| `git show 8ebaf8daad:Makefile \| grep -o 'PROJECT=[A-Za-z0-9_]*' \| sort \| uniq -c` | `27 PROJECT=ROCKNIX`, nothing else | a Makefile target builds another project, so its class (b) rows would be live |
| `scripts/unpack:120-160`, the patch directory lines | `PATCH_ARCH` set at `:127-133`; the globs at `:150-159` (`patches/*.patch`, `patches/${PATCH_ARCH}/`, the version and project/device forms) | -- (the rule the classes follow) |
| `git ls-tree -r --name-only 8ebaf8daad -- projects/ROCKNIX/packages \| grep /patches/x86_64/` | exactly the five files | another x86_64 directory, a sixth class (c) row |
| the header count over the 760 `.patch` | 746 with `From:` and `Subject:`; 14 without (5 s) | a header-less patch the table could not name |
| T23's units from its branch (`git show feature/m6-packages-dead-ends-670:...`) | 731 | T23's table was not the one accepted |
| `git cat-file -e upstream/next:<path>` over the 778 | 6 deleted (strace 2, grub 4) | -- (a third upstream status) |
| `git log --since=2026-06-01 8ebaf8daad -- <the 778>` | 102 commits: 99 LibreELEC's, 3 ROCKNIX's own; 0 not on `upstream/next` | a fork commit on a dead patch |
| `git ls-tree -r 8ebaf8daad \| awk '$1=="120000"'`, the `patches` entries | `gcc/patches` and `cmake/patches` link to the generic directories (and five more, outside the 778) | -- (the second inventory gap) |
| `tools/t40-tables.py <tree> stats` | 778 rows, the five classes 277 / 234 / 5 / 234 / 28, e 6 + 16 + 2 + 4; the generator asserts | a row the script cannot class, or a count that does not hold |
| the shadowed 74 against each override's `patches/` at three strictness levels | 2 strict duplicates; 12 re-offset; 6 with the context moved; 50 with no counterpart (each match has changed lines and an override file of the same name) | a duplicate miscounted, either way |

## Proofs

| Claim | Evidence | proved: |
| --- | --- | --- |
| The 778 and their counts | `stage-a/scope.out`; the generator's asserts | proved: tree |
| No `.patch`, `.conf`, recipe or tool changed; `packages/` and `projects/` equal `8ebaf8daad` | `git diff --stat` (`00`) | proved: history |
| Every device builds the ROCKNIX `linux` | `stage-a/linux.out` | proved: tool-run |
| No other project is built | the Makefile count | proved: tree |
| The five classes and the (e) split | `stage-a/stats.out` | proved: tool-run |
| `PATCH_ARCH=x86` never selects `patches/x86_64`, by design since 2014 | `unpack:127-128`; `git log -S'PATCH_ARCH="x86"'` (`711b5fc4c3`) | proved: tree |
| vitaquake2-lr's `x86_64` patch differs from `aarch64`'s by one hunk; `pre_make_target` names only aarch64 | the `diff` above; `package.mk:19-25` | proved: tree |
| `cpugov.patch` calls `get_available_cpu_governors` in `getAvailableGpuGovernors` | the patch's lines 17-24 | proved: tree |
| gcc's and cmake's `patches` are symlinks to the generic directories | `git ls-tree` mode `120000` and the blob's target | proved: tree |
| 746 headers and 14 hand-made, named | `stage-a/headers.out`, `stage-a/headerless.txt` | proved: tool-run |
| 6 deleted at `upstream/next` | `stage-a/deleted-upstream.txt` | proved: history |
| 102 commits since June, 99 + 3, 0 fork-authored | `stage-a/churn.txt` | proved: history |
| The shadowed 74's tiers | the generator; the match list (`02` § The shadowed 74) | proved: tool-run |
| Class (d) rows carry T23's dispositions | `stage-a/t23-units.txt`; the reach rows read from `f1561559aa` | proved: tree |
